Return a new dma-\/buf file descriptor for an exposed guest framebuffer described by the provided dmabuf\+\_\+id. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} dmabuf\+\_\+id is returned from V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+ D\+E\+V\+I\+C\+E\+\_\+\+Q\+U\+E\+R\+Y\+\_\+\+G\+F\+X\+\_\+\+P\+L\+A\+NE as a token of the exposed guest framebuffer. \mbox{\Hypertarget{uapi_2linux_2vfio_8h_ad4bd5a351953911686da15b972c7d9ab}\label{uapi_2linux_2vfio_8h_ad4bd5a351953911686da15b972c7d9ab}}

V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+G\+R\+O\+U\+P\+\_\+\+G\+E\+T\+\_\+\+D\+E\+V\+I\+C\+E\+\_\+\+FD -\/ \+\_\+\+I\+OW(V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+PE, V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+B\+A\+SE + 6, char)

Return a new file descriptor for the device object described by the provided string. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} string should match a device listed in the devices subdirectory of the I\+O\+M\+MU group sysfs entry. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} group containing the device must already be added to this context. Return\+: new file descriptor on success, -\/errno on failure. Availability\+: When attached to container \mbox{\Hypertarget{uapi_2linux_2vfio_8h_af94ac9c5bd1ae9737e687926f5f96a28}\label{uapi_2linux_2vfio_8h_af94ac9c5bd1ae9737e687926f5f96a28}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+G\+R\+O\+U\+P\+\_\+\+S\+E\+T\+\_\+\+C\+O\+N\+T\+A\+I\+N\+ER~\mbox{\hyperlink{include_2uapi_2asm-generic_2ioctl_8h_a840d10d2fed07b8a85f260031f2fa47b}{\+\_\+\+IO}}(\mbox{\hyperlink{uapi_2linux_2vfio_8h_a854f61b47968366825f68fefbcaf3b23}{V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+PE}}, \mbox{\hyperlink{uapi_2linux_2vfio_8h_ab71fbdc06d8c7dc2ab24f5dc0741e17e}{V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+B\+A\+SE}} + 4)}

V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+G\+R\+O\+U\+P\+\_\+\+S\+E\+T\+\_\+\+C\+O\+N\+T\+A\+I\+N\+ER -\/ \+\_\+\+I\+OW(V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+PE, V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+B\+A\+SE + 4, \+\_\+\+\_\+s32)

Set the container for the V\+F\+IO group to the open V\+F\+IO file descriptor provided. Groups may only belong to a single container. Containers may, at their discretion, support multiple groups. Only when a container is set are all of the interfaces of the V\+F\+IO file descriptor and the V\+F\+IO group file descriptor available to the user. Return\+: 0 on success, -\/errno on failure. Availability\+: Always \mbox{\Hypertarget{uapi_2linux_2vfio_8h_af46a65bdee456ed8139e169e24ba6189}\label{uapi_2linux_2vfio_8h_af46a65bdee456ed8139e169e24ba6189}} 
\index{vfio.h@{vfio.h}!VFIO\_GROUP\_UNSET\_CONTAINER@{VFIO\_GROUP\_UNSET\_CONTAINER}}
\index{VFIO\_GROUP\_UNSET\_CONTAINER@{VFIO\_GROUP\_UNSET\_CONTAINER}!vfio.h@{vfio.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{VFIO\_GROUP\_UNSET\_CONTAINER}{VFIO\_GROUP\_UNSET\_CONTAINER}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+G\+R\+O\+U\+P\+\_\+\+U\+N\+S\+E\+T\+\_\+\+C\+O\+N\+T\+A\+I\+N\+ER~\mbox{\hyperlink{include_2uapi_2asm-generic_2ioctl_8h_a840d10d2fed07b8a85f260031f2fa47b}{\+\_\+\+IO}}(\mbox{\hyperlink{uapi_2linux_2vfio_8h_a854f61b47968366825f68fefbcaf3b23}{V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+PE}}, \mbox{\hyperlink{uapi_2linux_2vfio_8h_ab71fbdc06d8c7dc2ab24f5dc0741e17e}{V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+B\+A\+SE}} + 5)}

V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+G\+R\+O\+U\+P\+\_\+\+U\+N\+S\+E\+T\+\_\+\+C\+O\+N\+T\+A\+I\+N\+ER -\/ \+\_\+\+IO(V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+PE, V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+B\+A\+SE + 5)

Remove the group from the attached container. This is the opposite of the S\+E\+T\+\_\+\+C\+O\+N\+T\+A\+I\+N\+ER call and returns the group to an initial state. All device file descriptors must be released prior to calling this interface. When removing the last group from a container, the I\+O\+M\+MU will be disabled and all state lost, effectively also returning the V\+F\+IO file descriptor to an initial state. Return\+: 0 on success, -\/errno on failure. Availability\+: When attached to container \mbox{\Hypertarget{uapi_2linux_2vfio_8h_a6621b14cf3bbd9016a67740e35b15df0}\label{uapi_2linux_2vfio_8h_a6621b14cf3bbd9016a67740e35b15df0}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{VFIO\_SET\_IOMMU}{VFIO\_SET\_IOMMU}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+S\+E\+T\+\_\+\+I\+O\+M\+MU~\mbox{\hyperlink{include_2uapi_2asm-generic_2ioctl_8h_a840d10d2fed07b8a85f260031f2fa47b}{\+\_\+\+IO}}(\mbox{\hyperlink{uapi_2linux_2vfio_8h_a854f61b47968366825f68fefbcaf3b23}{V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+PE}}, \mbox{\hyperlink{uapi_2linux_2vfio_8h_ab71fbdc06d8c7dc2ab24f5dc0741e17e}{V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+B\+A\+SE}} + 2)}

V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+S\+E\+T\+\_\+\+I\+O\+M\+MU -\/ \+\_\+\+I\+OW(V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+PE, V\+F\+I\+O\+\_\+\+B\+A\+SE + 2, \+\_\+\+\_\+s32)

Set the iommu to the given type. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} type must be supported by an iommu driver as verified by calling C\+H\+E\+C\+K\+\_\+\+E\+X\+T\+E\+N\+S\+I\+ON using the same type. A group must be set to this file descriptor before this ioctl is available. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} I\+O\+M\+MU interfaces enabled by this call are specific to the value set. Return\+: 0 on success, -\/errno on failure Availability\+: When V\+F\+IO group attached \mbox{\Hypertarget{uapi_2linux_2vfio_8h_a22e34e29b8aa84b01fdfb24ee6085a27}\label{uapi_2linux_2vfio_8h_a22e34e29b8aa84b01fdfb24ee6085a27}}
